					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content mw-content-ltr" lang="en" dir="ltr"><div class="mw-parser-output"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none">Country in the Lesser Antilles of the West Indies</div>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1033289096">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}</style><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">This article is about the sovereign country in West Indies. For the islands of the sovereign state, see <a href="/wiki/Antigua" title="Antigua">Antigua</a> and <a href="/wiki/Barbuda" title="Barbuda">Barbuda</a>. For other uses, see <a href="/wiki/Antigua_(disambiguation)" class="mw-disambig" title="Antigua (disambiguation)">Antigua (disambiguation)</a> and <a href="/wiki/Barbuda_(disambiguation)" class="mw-disambig" title="Barbuda (disambiguation)">Barbuda (disambiguation)</a>.</div>

in &#39;fleece&#39;">iː</span><span title="/ɡ/: &#39;g&#39; in &#39;guy&#39;">ɡ</span><span title="&#39;w&#39; in &#39;wind&#39;">w</span><span title="/ə/: &#39;a&#39; in &#39;about&#39;">ə</span></span>&#8201;...<span class="wrap">&#8201;</span><span style="border-bottom:1px dotted"><span title="&#39;b&#39; in &#39;buy&#39;">b</span><span title="/ɑːr/: &#39;ar&#39; in &#39;far&#39;">ɑːr</span><span title="/ˈ/: primary stress follows">ˈ</span><span title="&#39;b&#39; in &#39;buy&#39;">b</span><span title="/j/: &#39;y&#39; in &#39;yes&#39;">j</span><span title="/uː/: &#39;oo&#39; in &#39;goose&#39;">uː</span><span title="&#39;d&#39; in &#39;dye&#39;">d</span><span title="/ə/: &#39;a&#39; in &#39;about&#39;">ə</span></span>/</a></span></span>) is a <a href="/wiki/Sovereign_state" title="Sovereign state">sovereign</a> <a href="/wiki/Island_country" title="Island country">island country</a> in the <a href="/wiki/West_Indies" title="West Indies">West Indies</a>. It lies at the conjuncture of the <a href="/wiki/Caribbean_Sea" title="Caribbean Sea">Caribbean Sea</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Atlantic_Ocean" title="Atlantic Ocean">Atlantic Ocean</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Leeward_Islands" title="Leeward Islands">Leeward Islands</a> part of the <a href="/wiki/Lesser_Antilles" title="Lesser Antilles">Lesser Antilles</a>.
</p><p>The country consists of two major islands, <a href="/wiki/Antigua" title="Antigua">Antigua</a> and <a href="/wiki/Barbuda" title="Barbuda">Barbuda</a>, which are approximately 40&#160;km (20&#160;mi) apart, and several smaller islands, including <a href="/wiki/Great_Bird_Island,_Antigua" class="mw-redirect" title="Great Bird Island, Antigua">Great Bird</a>, <a href="/wiki/Green_Island_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="Green Island (Antigua and Barbuda)">Green</a>, <a href="/wiki/Guiana_Island" title="Guiana Island">Guiana</a>, <a href="/wiki/Long_Island_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="Long Island (Antigua and Barbuda)">Long</a>, <a href="/wiki/Maiden_Island_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="Maiden Island (Antigua and Barbuda)">Maiden</a>, <a href="/wiki/Prickly_Pear_Island" title="Prickly Pear Island">Prickly Pear</a>, <a href="/wiki/York_Island_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="York Island (Antigua and Barbuda)">York</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Redonda" title="Redonda">Redonda</a>. The permanent population is approximately 97,120 (2019<sup class="plainlinks noexcerpt noprint asof-tag update" style="display:none;"><a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit">&#91;update&#93;</a></sup> estimates), with 97% residing in Antigua.<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/St._John%27s,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="St. John&#39;s, Antigua and Barbuda">St. John's</a>, Antigua, is the country's capital, major city, and largest port. <a href="/wiki/Codrington,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Codrington, Antigua and Barbuda">Codrington</a> is Barbuda's largest town.
</p><p>In 1493, <a href="/wiki/Christopher_Columbus" title="Christopher Columbus">Christopher Columbus</a> surveyed the island of Antigua, which he named for the <a href="/wiki/Santa_Mar%C3%ADa_La_Antigua" title="Santa María La Antigua">Church of Santa María La Antigua</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Crocker,_John_1968_13-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Crocker,_John_1968-13">&#91;13&#93;</a></sup> Great Britain colonized Antigua in 1632 and <a href="/wiki/Barbuda" title="Barbuda">Barbuda</a> in 1678.<sup id="cite_ref-Crocker,_John_1968_13-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Crocker,_John_1968-13">&#91;13&#93;</a></sup> A part of the <a href="/wiki/British_Leeward_Islands" title="British Leeward Islands">Federal Colony of the Leeward Islands</a> from 1871, Antigua and Barbuda joined the <a href="/wiki/West_Indies_Federation" title="West Indies Federation">West Indies Federation</a> in 1958.<sup id="cite_ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14">&#91;14&#93;</a></sup> With the breakup of the federation in 1962, it became one of the <a href="/wiki/West_Indies_Associated_States" title="West Indies Associated States">West Indies Associated States</a> in 1967.<sup id="cite_ref-Sheridan1974_15-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Sheridan1974-15">&#91;15&#93;</a></sup> Following a period of internal self-governance, it gained full independence from the United Kingdom on 1 November 1981. Antigua and Barbuda is a member of the <a href="/wiki/Commonwealth_of_Nations" title="Commonwealth of Nations">Commonwealth</a> and a <a href="/wiki/Commonwealth_realm" title="Commonwealth realm">Commonwealth realm</a>; it is a <a href="/wiki/Constitutional_monarchy" title="Constitutional monarchy">constitutional monarchy</a> with <a href="/wiki/Charles_III" title="Charles III">Charles III</a> as its head of state.<sup id="cite_ref-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-16">&#91;16&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The <a href="/wiki/Economy_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Economy of Antigua and Barbuda">economy of Antigua and Barbuda</a> is largely dependent on tourism, which accounts for 80% of its GDP. <a href="/wiki/Effects_of_climate_change_on_island_nations" class="mw-redirect" title="Effects of climate change on island nations">Like other island nations</a>, Antigua and Barbuda is vulnerable to the <a href="/wiki/Effects_of_climate_change" title="Effects of climate change">effects of climate change</a>, such as <a href="/wiki/Sea_level_rise" title="Sea level rise">sea level rise</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Extreme_weather" title="Extreme weather">increased intensity of extreme weather</a> like <a href="/wiki/Hurricanes" class="mw-redirect" title="Hurricanes">hurricanes</a>. These cause <a href="/wiki/Coastal_erosion" title="Coastal erosion">coastal erosion</a>, <a href="/wiki/Water_scarcity" title="Water scarcity">water scarcity</a>, and other challenges.<sup id="cite_ref-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-17">&#91;17&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Antigua and Barbuda offers a <a href="/wiki/Citizenship_by_investment" class="mw-redirect" title="Citizenship by investment">citizenship by investment</a> program.<sup id="cite_ref-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-18">&#91;18&#93;</a></sup> The country levies no personal <a href="/wiki/Income_tax" title="Income tax">income tax</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-auto1_19-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-auto1-19">&#91;19&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Etymology">Etymology</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Etymology">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><span title="Spanish-language text"><i lang="es">Antigua</i></span> is <a href="/wiki/Spanish_language" title="Spanish language">Spanish</a> for 'ancient' and <span title="Spanish-language text"><i lang="es">barbuda</i></span> is Spanish for 'bearded'.<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> The island of Antigua was originally called <span title="Taino-language text"><i lang="tnq">Wadadli</i></span> by the <a href="/wiki/Arawak" title="Arawak">Arawaks</a> and is locally known by that name today; the <a href="/wiki/Island_Caribs" class="mw-redirect" title="Island Caribs">Caribs</a> possibly called Barbuda <span title="Island Carib-language text"><i lang="crb">Wa'omoni</i></span>. <a href="/wiki/Christopher_Columbus" title="Christopher Columbus">Christopher Columbus</a>, while sailing by in 1493, may have named it <span title="Spanish-language text"><i lang="es">Santa Maria la Antigua</i></span>, after an icon in the Spanish <a href="/wiki/Seville_Cathedral" title="Seville Cathedral">Seville Cathedral</a>. The "bearded" of Barbuda is thought to refer either to the male inhabitants of the island, or the bearded fig trees present there.<sup id="cite_ref-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-20">&#91;20&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="History">History</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: History">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/History_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="History of Antigua and Barbuda">History of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Pre-colonial_period">Pre-colonial period</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Pre-colonial period">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Antigua was first settled by <a href="/wiki/Archaic_period_(North_America)" title="Archaic period (North America)">archaic age</a> hunter-gatherer <a href="/wiki/Amerindian" class="mw-redirect" title="Amerindian">Amerindians</a> called the <a href="/wiki/Ciboney" title="Ciboney">Ciboney</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Radiocarbon_dating" title="Radiocarbon dating">Carbon dating</a> has established the earliest settlements started around 3100 BC.<sup id="cite_ref-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-23">&#91;23&#93;</a></sup> They were succeeded by the ceramic age pre-Columbian <a href="/wiki/Arawakan_languages" title="Arawakan languages">Arawak</a>-speaking <a href="/wiki/Saladoid" title="Saladoid">Saladoid</a> people who migrated from the lower <a href="/wiki/Orinoco_River" class="mw-redirect" title="Orinoco River">Orinoco River</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-24">&#91;24&#93;</a></sup> They introduced agriculture, raising, among other crops, the famous <a href="/wiki/Antigua_Black_Pineapple" class="mw-redirect" title="Antigua Black Pineapple">Antigua black pineapple</a> (<i><a href="/wiki/Ananas_comosus" class="mw-redirect" title="Ananas comosus">Ananas comosus</a></i>), <a href="/wiki/Maize" title="Maize">corn</a>, <a href="/wiki/Sweet_potato" title="Sweet potato">sweet potatoes</a>, <a href="/wiki/Chili_pepper" title="Chili pepper">chiles</a>, <a href="/wiki/Guava" title="Guava">guava</a>, <a href="/wiki/Tobacco" title="Tobacco">tobacco</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Cotton" title="Cotton">cotton</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-25">&#91;25&#93;</a></sup> Later on the more bellicose <a href="/wiki/Island_Caribs" class="mw-redirect" title="Island Caribs">Caribs</a> also settled the island, possibly by force.
</p>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_(1823),_plate_V_-_BL.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/01/The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg/220px-The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="153" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/01/The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg/330px-The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/01/The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg/440px-The_Mill_Yard_-_Ten_Views_in_the_Island_of_Antigua_%281823%29%2C_plate_V_-_BL.jpg 2x" data-file-width="1784" data-file-height="1241" /></a><figcaption>Antigua in 1823</figcaption></figure>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="European_arrival_and_settlement">European arrival and settlement</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: European arrival and settlement">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/wiki/Christopher_Columbus" title="Christopher Columbus">Christopher Columbus</a> was the first European to sight the islands in 1493.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup> The Spanish did not colonise Antigua until after a combination of European and African diseases, malnutrition, and slavery eventually extirpated most of the native population; <a href="/wiki/Smallpox" title="Smallpox">smallpox</a> was probably the greatest killer.<sup id="cite_ref-26" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-26">&#91;26&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The English settled on Antigua in 1632;<sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Christopher Codrington settled on Barbuda in 1685.<sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Tobacco and then sugar was grown, worked by a large population of slaves transported from West Africa, who soon came to vastly outnumber the European settlers.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Colonial_era">Colonial era</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Colonial era">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The English maintained control of the islands, repulsing an attempted French attack in 1666.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> The brutal conditions endured by the slaves led to revolts in 1701 and 1729 and a planned revolt in 1736, the last led by <a href="/wiki/Prince_Klaas" title="Prince Klaas">Prince Klaas</a>, though it was discovered before it began and the ringleaders were executed.<sup id="cite_ref-27" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-27">&#91;27&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Slavery" title="Slavery">Slavery</a> was abolished in the British Empire in 1833, affecting the economy.<sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> This was exacerbated by natural disasters such as the 1843 earthquake and the 1847 hurricane.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Mining occurred on the isle of <a href="/wiki/Redonda" title="Redonda">Redonda</a>, however, this ceased in 1929 and the island has since remained uninhabited.<sup id="cite_ref-kras-2008_28-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-kras-2008-28">&#91;28&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Part of the Leeward Islands colony, Antigua and Barbuda became part of the short-lived <a href="/wiki/West_Indies_Federation" title="West Indies Federation">West Indies Federation</a> from 1958 to 1962.<sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Antigua and Barbuda subsequently became an associated state of the United Kingdom with full internal autonomy on 27 February 1967.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> The 1970s were dominated by discussions as to the islands' future and the rivalry between <a href="/wiki/Vere_Bird" title="Vere Bird">Vere Bird</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Labour_Party" title="Antigua and Barbuda Labour Party">Antigua and Barbuda Labour Party</a> (ABLP) (Premier from 1967 to 1971 and 1976 to 1981) and the <a href="/wiki/Progressive_Labour_Movement" title="Progressive Labour Movement">Progressive Labour Movement</a> (PLM) of <a href="/wiki/George_Walter" title="George Walter">George Walter</a> (Premier 1971–1976). Eventually, Antigua and Barbuda gained full independence on 1 November 1981; Vere Bird became prime minister of the new country.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> The country opted to remain within the <a href="/wiki/Commonwealth_of_Nations" title="Commonwealth of Nations">Commonwealth</a>, retaining Queen Elizabeth as <a href="/wiki/Monarchy_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Monarchy of Antigua and Barbuda">head of state</a>, with the last <a href="/wiki/List_of_colonial_governors_and_administrators_of_Antigua#Governors_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda_(1967–1981)" title="List of colonial governors and administrators of Antigua">governor</a>, <a href="/wiki/Wilfred_Jacobs" title="Wilfred Jacobs">Sir Wilfred Jacobs</a>, as <a href="/wiki/Governor-general_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Governor-general of Antigua and Barbuda">governor-general</a>.
</p>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/90/1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg/280px-1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg" decoding="async" width="280" height="212" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/90/1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg/420px-1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/90/1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg/560px-1953_Antigua_stamps.jpg 2x" data-file-width="3255" data-file-height="2459" /></a><figcaption>The Queen on 1953 Antiguan stamps</figcaption></figure>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Independence_era">Independence era</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Independence era">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The first two decades of Antigua's independence were dominated politically by the Bird family and the ABLP, with <a href="/wiki/Vere_Bird" title="Vere Bird">Vere Bird</a> ruling from 1981 to 1994, followed by his son <a href="/wiki/Lester_Bird" title="Lester Bird">Lester Bird</a> from 1994 to 2004.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Though providing a degree of political stability, and boosting tourism to the country, the Bird governments were frequently accused of corruption, cronyism and financial malfeasance.<sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Vere_Bird_Jr." title="Vere Bird Jr.">Vere Bird Jr.</a>, the elder son, was forced to leave the cabinet in 1990 following a <a href="/wiki/Guns_for_Antigua" title="Guns for Antigua">scandal</a> in which he was accused of smuggling Israeli weapons to Colombian drug-traffickers.<sup id="cite_ref-29" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-29">&#91;29&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-30" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-30">&#91;30&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Commonwealth_22-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Commonwealth-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup> Another son, <a href="/wiki/Ivor_Bird" title="Ivor Bird">Ivor Bird</a>, was convicted of selling <a href="/wiki/Cocaine" title="Cocaine">cocaine</a> in 1995.<sup id="cite_ref-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-31">&#91;31&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-32" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-32">&#91;32&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In 1995, <a href="/wiki/Hurricane_Luis" title="Hurricane Luis">Hurricane Luis</a> caused severe damage on Barbuda.<sup id="cite_ref-33" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-33">&#91;33&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The ABLP's dominance of Antiguan politics ended with the <a href="/wiki/2004_Antiguan_general_election" title="2004 Antiguan general election">2004 Antiguan general election</a>, which was won by <a href="/wiki/Winston_Baldwin_Spencer" class="mw-redirect" title="Winston Baldwin Spencer">Winston Baldwin Spencer</a>'s <a href="/wiki/United_Progressive_Party_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="United Progressive Party (Antigua and Barbuda)">United Progressive Party</a> (UPP).<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Winston Baldwin Spencer was <a href="/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Prime Minister of Antigua and Barbuda">Prime Minister of Antigua and Barbuda</a> from 2004 to 2014.<sup id="cite_ref-34" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-34">&#91;34&#93;</a></sup> However the UPP lost the <a href="/wiki/2014_Antiguan_general_election" title="2014 Antiguan general election">2014 Antiguan general election</a>, with the ABLP returning to power under <a href="/wiki/Gaston_Browne" title="Gaston Browne">Gaston Browne</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-bald_35-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-bald-35">&#91;35&#93;</a></sup> ABLP won 15 of the 17 seats in the <a href="/wiki/2018_Antiguan_general_election" title="2018 Antiguan general election">2018 snap election</a> under the leadership of incumbent Prime Minister Gaston Browne.<sup id="cite_ref-36" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-36">&#91;36&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In 2016, <a href="/wiki/Nelson%27s_Dockyard" title="Nelson&#39;s Dockyard">Nelson's Dockyard</a> was designated as a <a href="/wiki/UNESCO_World_Heritage_Site" class="mw-redirect" title="UNESCO World Heritage Site">UNESCO World Heritage Site</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-37" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-37">&#91;37&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Most of Barbuda was devastated in early September 2017 by <a href="/wiki/Hurricane_Irma" title="Hurricane Irma">Hurricane Irma</a>, which brought winds with speeds reaching 295&#160;km/h (185&#160;mph). The storm damaged or destroyed 95% of the island's buildings and infrastructure, leaving Barbuda "barely habitable" according to Prime Minister Gaston Browne. Nearly everyone on the island was evacuated to Antigua.<sup id="cite_ref-auto_38-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-auto-38">&#91;38&#93;</a></sup>
Amidst the following rebuilding efforts on Barbuda that were estimated to cost at least $100 million,<sup id="cite_ref-time.com_39-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-time.com-39">&#91;39&#93;</a></sup> the government announced plans to revoke a century-old law of <a href="/wiki/Communal_land" title="Communal land">communal land ownership</a> by allowing residents to buy land; a move that has been criticised as promoting "disaster capitalism".<sup id="cite_ref-NACLA-disaster-capitalism_40-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-NACLA-disaster-capitalism-40">&#91;40&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Geography">Geography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Geography">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Geography_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Geography of Antigua and Barbuda">Geography of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1021810730">@media all and (max-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery{width:100%!important}}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery{display:table}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-default{background:transparent;margin-top:0.3em}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-center{margin-left:auto;margin-right:auto}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-left{float:left}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-right{float:right}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-none{float:none}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery-collapsible{width:100%}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .title,.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .main,.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .footer{display:table-row}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .title>div{display:table-cell;padding:0.2em 0 0.6em 1.6em;text-align:center;font-weight:bold}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .main>div{display:table-cell}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .gallery{line-height:1.35em}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .footer>div{display:table-cell;padding:0.2em 0 0.6em 1.6em;text-align:right;font-size:80%;line-height:1em}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .title>div *,.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .footer>div *{overflow:visible}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .gallerybox img{background:none!important}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .bordered-images .thumb img{border:solid #eaecf0 1px}.mw-parser-output .mod-gallery .whitebg .thumb{background:#fff!important}</style><div class="mod-gallery mod-gallery-default mod-gallery-right"><div class="main"><div><ul class="gallery mw-gallery-traditional nochecker bordered-images whitebg">

<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r997900035">.mw-parser-output .locmap .od{position:absolute}.mw-parser-output .locmap .id{position:absolute;line-height:0}.mw-parser-output .locmap .l0{font-size:0;position:absolute}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pv{line-height:110%;position:absolute;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pl{line-height:110%;position:absolute;top:-0.75em;text-align:right}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pr{line-height:110%;position:absolute;top:-0.75em;text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pv>div{display:inline;padding:1px}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pl>div{display:inline;padding:1px;float:right}.mw-parser-output .locmap .pr>div{display:inline;padding:1px;float:left}</style><div class="locmap noviewer noresize thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:302px"><div style="position:relative;width:300px;border:1px solid lightgray"><span class="notpageimage" /></a></span><div class="od" style="top:80.343%;left:78.844%"><div class="id" style="left:-4px;top:-4px"><span class="notpageimage" typeof="mw:File"><span title="Antigua"><img alt="Antigua" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/8px-Red_pog.svg.png" decoding="async" width="8" height="8" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/12px-Red_pog.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/16px-Red_pog.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></span></span></div><div class="pl" style="font-size:91%;width:6em;right:5px"><div><a href="/wiki/Antigua" title="Antigua">Antigua</a></div></div></div><div class="od" style="top:16.547%;left:78.844%"><div class="id" style="left:-4px;top:-4px"><span class="notpageimage" typeof="mw:File"><span title="Barbuda"><img alt="Barbuda" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/8px-Red_pog.svg.png" decoding="async" width="8" height="8" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/12px-Red_pog.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/16px-Red_pog.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></span></span></div><div class="pl" style="font-size:91%;width:6em;right:5px"><div><a href="/wiki/Barbuda" title="Barbuda">Barbuda</a></div></div></div><div class="od" style="top:97.621%;left:7.665%"><div class="id" style="left:-4px;top:-4px"><span class="notpageimage" typeof="mw:File"><span title="Redonda"><img alt="Redonda" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/8px-Red_pog.svg.png" decoding="async" width="8" height="8" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/12px-Red_pog.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0c/Red_pog.svg/16px-Red_pog.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></span></span></div><div class="pr" style="font-size:91%;width:6em;left:5px"><div><a href="/wiki/Redonda" title="Redonda">Redonda</a></div></div></div></div><div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua_and_Barbuda_location_map.svg" title="File:Antigua and Barbuda location map.svg">class=notpageimage| </a></div>Antigua and Barbuda</div></div></div>
<p>Limestone formations, rather than volcanic activity, have had the most impact on the topography of both Antigua and Barbuda, which are both relatively low-lying islands. Boggy Peak, also known as Mt. Obama from 2008 to 2016, is the highest point on both Antigua and Barbuda. It is the remnant of a volcanic crater and rises a total of 402 meters. Boggy Peak is located in the southwest of Antigua (1,319 feet).<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Both of these islands have very irregularly shaped coastlines that are dotted with beaches, lagoons, and natural harbors. There are reefs and shoals that surround the islands on all sides. Because of the low amount of rainfall, there are not many streams. On neither of these islands can sufficient quantities of fresh groundwater be found.<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Redonda is a small, uninhabited island located about 40 kilometers (25 miles) to the south-west of Antigua. Redonda is a rocky island.<sup id="cite_ref-Britannica_21-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Britannica-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Cities_and_villages">Cities and villages</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Cities and villages">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Further information: <a href="/wiki/List_of_cities_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="List of cities in Antigua and Barbuda">List of cities in Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<p>The most populous cities in Antigua and Barbuda are mostly on Antigua, being <a href="/wiki/Saint_John%27s,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Saint John&#39;s, Antigua and Barbuda">Saint John's</a>, <a href="/wiki/All_Saints,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="All Saints, Antigua and Barbuda">All Saints</a>, <a href="/wiki/Piggotts" title="Piggotts">Piggotts</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Liberta,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Liberta, Antigua and Barbuda">Liberta</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-41" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-41">&#91;41&#93;</a></sup> The most populous city on Barbuda is <a href="/wiki/Codrington,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Codrington, Antigua and Barbuda">Codrington</a>. It is estimated that 25% of the population lives in an urban area, which is much lower than the international average of 55%.<sup id="cite_ref-42" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-42">&#91;42&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-43" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-43">&#91;43&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Islands">Islands</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Islands">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Further information: <a href="/wiki/Geography_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda#Islands" title="Geography of Antigua and Barbuda">Geography of Antigua and Barbuda §&#160;Islands</a></div>
<p>Antigua and Barbuda consists mostly of its two namesake islands, <a href="/wiki/Antigua" title="Antigua">Antigua</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Barbuda" title="Barbuda">Barbuda</a>. Other than that, Antigua and Barbuda's biggest islands are Guiana Island and Long Island off the coast of Antigua, and <a href="/wiki/Redonda" title="Redonda">Redonda</a> island, which is far from both of the main islands.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Climate">Climate</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Climate">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Rainfall averages 990&#160;mm (39&#160;in) per year, with the amount varying widely from season to season. In general the wettest period is between September and November. The islands generally experience low humidity and recurrent droughts. Temperatures average 27&#160;°C (80.6&#160;°F), with a range from 23&#160;°C (73.4&#160;°F) to 29&#160;°C (84.2&#160;°F) in the winter to from 25&#160;°C (77.0&#160;°F) to 30&#160;°C (86.0&#160;°F) in the summer and autumn. The coolest period is between December and February.
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Hurricane" class="mw-redirect" title="Hurricane">Hurricanes</a> strike on an average of once a year, including the powerful <a href="/wiki/Category_5_hurricane" class="mw-redirect" title="Category 5 hurricane">Category 5</a> <a href="/wiki/Hurricane_Irma" title="Hurricane Irma">Hurricane Irma</a>, on 6 September 2017, which damaged 95% of the structures on Barbuda.<sup id="cite_ref-44" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-44">&#91;44&#93;</a></sup> Some 1,800 people were evacuated to Antigua.<sup id="cite_ref-45" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-45">&#91;45&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>An estimate published by <i><a href="/wiki/Time_(magazine)" title="Time (magazine)">Time</a></i> indicated that over $100&#160;million would be required to rebuild homes and infrastructure. Philmore Mullin, Director of Barbuda's National Office of Disaster Services, said that "all critical infrastructure and utilities are non-existent – food supply, medicine, shelter, electricity, water, communications, waste management". He summarised the situation as follows: "Public utilities need to be rebuilt in their entirety... It is optimistic to think anything can be rebuilt in six months ... In my 25 years in disaster management, I have never seen something like this."<sup id="cite_ref-46" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-46">&#91;46&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Environmental_issues">Environmental issues</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Environmental issues">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="excerpt-block"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1066933788">.mw-parser-output .excerpt-hat .mw-editsection-like{font-style:normal}</style><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable dablink excerpt-hat selfref">This section is an excerpt from <a href="/wiki/Environmental_issues_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Environmental issues in Antigua and Barbuda">Environmental issues in Antigua and Barbuda</a>.<span class="mw-editsection-like plainlinks"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Environmental_issues_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div><div class="excerpt">
<p>Like other <a href="/wiki/Island_country" title="Island country">island nations</a>, Antigua and Barbuda faces unique environmental issues created by its proximity to the ocean, and small size. These include pressures on water resources, natural ecosystems, and deforestation more generally.  
</p><p>Existing issues on the island are further made worse by climate change, where, unlike other <a href="/wiki/Effects_of_climate_change_on_island_nations" class="mw-redirect" title="Effects of climate change on island nations">island nations affected by climate change</a>, <a href="/wiki/Sea_level_rise" title="Sea level rise">sea level rise</a>, increased weather variability, create increased pressures on the communities on the islands and the land, through processes like <a href="/wiki/Coastal_erosion" title="Coastal erosion">coastal erosion</a> and <a href="/wiki/Saltwater_intrusion" title="Saltwater intrusion">saltwater intrusion</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-47" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-47">&#91;47&#93;</a></sup>  
</p>
Not only do these issues threaten the residents of the island, but also interfere with the economy&#160;– where tourism is 80% of the GDP.<sup id="cite_ref-Environmental_issues_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda_:1_48-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Environmental_issues_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda_:1-48">&#91;48&#93;</a></sup> The 2017 hurricane season was particularly destructive, with <a href="/wiki/Hurricane_Maria" title="Hurricane Maria">Hurricane Maria</a> and <a href="/wiki/Hurricane_Irma" title="Hurricane Irma">Hurricane Irma</a>, repeatedly damaging vulnerable infrastructure on the islands of Antigua and Barbuda.<sup id="cite_ref-49" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-49">&#91;49&#93;</a></sup></div></div>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Demographics">Demographics</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Demographics">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Demographics_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Demographics of Antigua and Barbuda">Demographics of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c1/Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png/290px-Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png" decoding="async" width="290" height="171" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c1/Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png/435px-Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c1/Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png/580px-Antigua-Barbuda-demography.png 2x" data-file-width="724" data-file-height="426" /></a><figcaption>Antigua &amp; Barbuda's population (1961–2010). Number of inhabitants in thousands.</figcaption></figure>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Ethnic_groups">Ethnic groups</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Ethnic groups">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Antigua has a population of 93,219,<sup id="cite_ref-UN_WPP_50-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-UN_WPP-50">&#91;50&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-UN_WPP_2022_51-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-UN_WPP_2022-51">&#91;51&#93;</a></sup> mostly made up of people of <a href="/wiki/West_African" class="mw-redirect" title="West African">West African</a>, British, and <a href="/wiki/Portuguese_people" title="Portuguese people">Portuguese</a> descent. The ethnic distribution consists of 91% Black, 4.4% mixed race, 1.7% White, and 2.9% other (primarily <a href="/wiki/South_Asians" class="mw-redirect" title="South Asians">East Indian</a>). Most Whites are of British descent. Christian Levantine Arabs and a small number of <a href="/wiki/East_Asians" class="mw-redirect" title="East Asians">East Asians</a> and <a href="/wiki/Sephardic_Jews" title="Sephardic Jews">Sephardic Jews</a> make up the remainder of the population.
</p><p>An increasingly large percentage of the population lives abroad, most notably in the United Kingdom (<a href="/wiki/Antiguan_British" class="mw-redirect" title="Antiguan British">Antiguan Britons</a>), the United States and Canada. A minority of Antiguan residents are immigrants from other countries, particularly from Dominica, Guyana and <a href="/wiki/Jamaica" title="Jamaica">Jamaica</a>, and, increasingly, from the Dominican Republic, <a href="/wiki/St._Vincent_and_the_Grenadines" class="mw-redirect" title="St. Vincent and the Grenadines">St. Vincent and the Grenadines</a> and <a href="/wiki/Nigeria" title="Nigeria">Nigeria</a>. An estimated 4,500 American citizens also make their home in Antigua and Barbuda, making their numbers one of the largest American populations in the English-speaking Eastern Caribbean.<sup id="cite_ref-52" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-52">&#91;52&#93;</a></sup> 68.47% of the population was born in Antigua and Barbuda.<sup id="cite_ref-53" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-53">&#91;53&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Languages">Languages</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Languages">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The language most commonly used in business is English. There is a noticeable distinction between the Antiguan accent and the Barbudan one.
</p><p>When compared to Antiguan Creole, Standard English was the language of choice in the years leading up to Antigua and Barbuda's attainment of their independence. The Antiguan Creole language is looked down upon by the upper and middle classes in general. The Antiguan Creole language is discouraged from use in the educational system, and instruction is carried out in Standard (British) English instead.
</p><p>A significant number of the words that are utilized in the Antiguan dialect are derived from both the British and African languages. This is readily apparent in phrases such as "Innit?" which literally translates to "Isn't it?" Many common island proverbs can be traced back to Africa, such as the pidgin language.
</p><p>Approximately 10,000 people are able to speak in Spanish.<sup id="cite_ref-54" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-54">&#91;54&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Religion">Religion</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Religion">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1021810730"><div class="mod-gallery mod-gallery-default mod-gallery-right"><div class="main"><div><ul class="gallery mw-gallery-traditional nochecker bordered-images whitebg">
		<li class="gallerybox" style="width: 235px">

<p>A majority (77%)<sup id="cite_ref-Factbook_1-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Factbook-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> of Antiguans are Christians, with the <a href="/wiki/Anglicans" class="mw-redirect" title="Anglicans">Anglicans</a> (17.6%) being the largest single denomination. Other Christian denominations present are <a href="/wiki/Seventh-day_Adventist_Church" title="Seventh-day Adventist Church">Seventh-day Adventist Church</a> (12.4%), <a href="/wiki/Pentecostalism" title="Pentecostalism">Pentecostalism</a> (12.2%), <a href="/wiki/Moravian_Church" title="Moravian Church">Moravian Church</a> (8.3%), <a href="/wiki/Roman_Catholics" class="mw-redirect" title="Roman Catholics">Roman Catholics</a>
(8.2%), <a href="/wiki/Methodist_Church" class="mw-redirect" title="Methodist Church">Methodist Church</a> (5.6%), <a href="/wiki/Wesleyan_Holiness_Church" title="Wesleyan Holiness Church">Wesleyan Holiness Church</a> (4.5%), <a href="/wiki/Church_of_God_(Cleveland,_Tennessee)" title="Church of God (Cleveland, Tennessee)">Church of God</a> (4.1%), <a href="/wiki/Baptists" title="Baptists">Baptists</a> (3.6%),<sup id="cite_ref-FactbookAttribution_55-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-FactbookAttribution-55">&#91;55&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Mormonism" title="Mormonism">Mormonism</a> (&lt;1.0%), as well as <a href="/wiki/Jehovah%27s_Witnesses" title="Jehovah&#39;s Witnesses">Jehovah's Witnesses</a>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Governance">Governance</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Governance">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Political_system">Political system</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Political system">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Politics_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Politics of Antigua and Barbuda">Politics of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Monarchy_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Monarchy of Antigua and Barbuda">Monarchy of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/71/St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg/220px-St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="133" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/71/St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg/330px-St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/71/St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg/440px-St_Johns_Antigua_2012.jpg 2x" data-file-width="5421" data-file-height="3278" /></a><figcaption>St. John's on Antigua.</figcaption></figure>
<p>The politics of Antigua and Barbuda take place within a framework of a <a href="/wiki/Unitary_state" title="Unitary state">unitary</a>, <a href="/wiki/Parliamentary_system" title="Parliamentary system">parliamentary</a>, <a href="/wiki/Representative_democracy" title="Representative democracy">representative democratic</a> monarchy, in which the <a href="/wiki/Head_of_state" title="Head of state">head of state</a> is the monarch who appoints the <a href="/wiki/List_of_governors-general_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="List of governors-general of Antigua and Barbuda">governor-general</a> as vice-regal representative.<sup id="cite_ref-56" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-56">&#91;56&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Charles_III" title="Charles III">Charles III</a> is the present <a href="/wiki/Queen_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Queen of Antigua and Barbuda">King of Antigua and Barbuda</a>, having served in that position since the death of his mother, <a href="/wiki/Elizabeth_II" title="Elizabeth II">Elizabeth II</a>. She had been the queen since the islands' independence from the United Kingdom in 1981. The King is currently represented by Governor-General <a href="/wiki/Rodney_Williams_(governor-general)" title="Rodney Williams (governor-general)">Sir Rodney Williams</a>. A council of ministers is appointed by the governor-general on the advice of the <a href="/wiki/List_of_prime_ministers_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="List of prime ministers of Antigua and Barbuda">prime minister</a>, currently <a href="/wiki/Gaston_Browne" title="Gaston Browne">Gaston Browne</a> (2014–).<sup id="cite_ref-FactbookAttribution_55-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-FactbookAttribution-55">&#91;55&#93;</a></sup> The prime minister is the <a href="/wiki/Head_of_government" title="Head of government">head of government</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-57" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-57">&#91;57&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Executive_power" class="mw-redirect" title="Executive power">Executive power</a> is exercised by the government while <a href="/wiki/Legislative_power" class="mw-redirect" title="Legislative power">legislative power</a> is vested in both the <a href="/wiki/Forms_of_government" class="mw-redirect" title="Forms of government">government</a> and the two chambers of <a href="/wiki/Parliament_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Parliament of Antigua and Barbuda">Parliament</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Bicameral" class="mw-redirect" title="Bicameral">bicameral</a> Parliament consists of the Senate (17 members appointed by members of the government and the <a href="/wiki/Official_Opposition" class="mw-redirect" title="Official Opposition">opposition</a> party, and approved by the governor-general), and the House of Representatives (17 members elected by <a href="/wiki/First_past_the_post" class="mw-redirect" title="First past the post">first past the post</a>) to serve five-year terms.
</p><p>The current Leader of His Majesty's Loyal Opposition is Jamale Pringle.
</p><p>There has been recent development in the <a href="/wiki/Republicanism_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Republicanism in Antigua and Barbuda">republicanism movement in Antigua and Barbuda</a>, following <a href="/wiki/Barbados" title="Barbados">Barbados</a> becoming a republic in 2021 and following the death of Elizabeth II in 2022, with an opinion poll showing majority support for the change.<sup id="cite_ref-58" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-58">&#91;58&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-:0_59-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-:0-59">&#91;59&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-60" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-60">&#91;60&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Elections">Elections</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Elections">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/66/Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg/220px-Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/66/Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg/330px-Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/66/Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg/440px-Antigua.St.John.from_west.wmt.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2000" data-file-height="1501" /></a><figcaption>St. John's parish on Antigua.</figcaption></figure>
<p>The last <a href="/wiki/2018_Antiguan_general_election" title="2018 Antiguan general election">election</a> was held on 21 March 2018. The Antigua Barbuda Labour Party (ABLP) led by Prime Minister Gaston Browne won 15 of the 17 seats in the House of Representatives.<sup id="cite_ref-61" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-61">&#91;61&#93;</a></sup> The previous <a href="/wiki/2014_Antiguan_general_election" title="2014 Antiguan general election">election</a> was on 12 June 2014, during which the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_Labour_Party" class="mw-redirect" title="Antigua Labour Party">Antigua Labour Party</a> won 14 seats, and the <a href="/wiki/United_Progressive_Party_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="United Progressive Party (Antigua and Barbuda)">United Progressive Party</a> 3 seats.<sup id="cite_ref-62" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-62">&#91;62&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Since 1951, elections have been won by the <a href="/wiki/Populism" title="Populism">populist</a> Antigua Labour Party. However, in the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_general_election,_2004" class="mw-redirect" title="Antigua and Barbuda general election, 2004">Antigua and Barbuda legislative election of 2004</a> saw the defeat of the longest-serving elected government in the Caribbean.<sup id="cite_ref-63" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-63">&#91;63&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Vere_Bird" title="Vere Bird">Vere Bird</a> was prime minister from 1981 to 1994 and chief minister of Antigua from 1960 to 1981, except for the 1971–1976 period when the Progressive Labour Movement (PLM) defeated his party. Bird, the nation's first prime minister, is credited with having brought Antigua and Barbuda and the Caribbean into a new era of independence. Prime Minister <a href="/wiki/Lester_Bird" title="Lester Bird">Lester Bryant Bird</a> succeeded the elder Bird in 1994.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Party_elections">Party elections</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Party elections">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p><a href="/wiki/Gaston_Browne" title="Gaston Browne">Gaston Browne</a> defeated his predecessor <a href="/wiki/Lester_Bryant_Bird" class="mw-redirect" title="Lester Bryant Bird">Lester Bryant Bird</a> at the Antigua Labour Party's biennial convention in November 2012 held to elect a political leader and other officers.<sup id="cite_ref-64" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-64">&#91;64&#93;</a></sup> The party then altered its name from the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_Labour_Party" class="mw-redirect" title="Antigua Labour Party">Antigua Labour Party</a> (ALP) to the Antigua and Barbuda Labour Party (ABLP). This was done to officially include the party's presence on the sister island of Barbuda in its organisation, the only political party on the mainland to have a physical branch in Barbuda.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Judiciary">Judiciary</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Judiciary">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Caribbean_Supreme_Court" title="Eastern Caribbean Supreme Court">Eastern Caribbean Supreme Court</a> is the highest court in the region's judicial system (based in Saint Lucia; one judge of the Supreme Court is a resident of the islands and presides over the High Court of Justice). The <a href="/wiki/Caribbean_Court_of_Justice" title="Caribbean Court of Justice">Caribbean Court of Justice</a> counts Antigua as one of its member states. Its highest court of appeal is the <a href="/wiki/Judicial_Committee_of_the_Privy_Council" title="Judicial Committee of the Privy Council">Judicial Committee of the Privy Council</a>, which acts in that capacity.<sup id="cite_ref-65" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-65">&#91;65&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Foreign_relations">Foreign relations</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Foreign relations">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Foreign relations of Antigua and Barbuda">Foreign relations of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/10/Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg/200px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg" decoding="async" width="200" height="356" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/10/Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg/300px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/10/Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg/400px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_embassy_in_Athens.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2250" data-file-height="4000" /></a><figcaption>The embassy of Antigua and Barbuda in Athens, Greece.</figcaption></figure>
<p>Antigua and Barbuda is a member of the United Nations, the <a href="/wiki/Bolivarian_Alliance_for_the_Americas" class="mw-redirect" title="Bolivarian Alliance for the Americas">Bolivarian Alliance for the Americas</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Commonwealth_of_Nations" title="Commonwealth of Nations">Commonwealth of Nations</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Caribbean_Community" title="Caribbean Community">Caribbean Community</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Organization_of_Eastern_Caribbean_States" class="mw-redirect" title="Organization of Eastern Caribbean States">Organization of Eastern Caribbean States</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Organization_of_American_States" title="Organization of American States">Organization of American States</a>, the <a href="/wiki/World_Trade_Organization" title="World Trade Organization">World Trade Organization</a> and the Eastern Caribbean's Regional Security System.
</p><p>Antigua and Barbuda is also a member of the <a href="/wiki/International_Criminal_Court" title="International Criminal Court">International Criminal Court</a> (with a <a href="/wiki/Bilateral_Immunity_Agreement" class="mw-redirect" title="Bilateral Immunity Agreement">Bilateral Immunity Agreement</a> of Protection for the US military as covered under Article 98 of the <a href="/wiki/Rome_Statute_of_the_International_Criminal_Court" class="mw-redirect" title="Rome Statute of the International Criminal Court">Rome Statute</a>).<sup id="cite_ref-66" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-66">&#91;66&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In 2013, Antigua and Barbuda called for <a href="/wiki/Reparations_for_slavery" title="Reparations for slavery">reparations for slavery</a> at the United Nations. Prime Minister <a href="/wiki/Baldwin_Spencer_(politician)" title="Baldwin Spencer (politician)">Baldwin Spencer</a> said "We have recently seen a number of leaders apologising", and that they should now "match their words with concrete and material benefits."<sup id="cite_ref-67" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-67">&#91;67&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Military">Military</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: Military">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Further information: <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Defence_Force" title="Antigua and Barbuda Defence Force">Antigua and Barbuda Defence Force</a></div>
<p>About 260 people are currently serving in the Antigua and Barbuda Defense Force in a variety of capacities. These personnel are distributed across the line infantry <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Regiment" title="Antigua and Barbuda Regiment">regiment</a>, the service and support unit, the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Air_Wing" title="Antigua and Barbuda Air Wing">air force</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Coast_Guard" title="Antigua and Barbuda Coast Guard">coast guard</a>. In addition to that, there is something called the Antigua and Barbuda Cadet Corps, which is made up of two hundred young people between the ages of 12 and 18. The Defence Board is in charge of directing the activities of the armed forces of the nation. The <a href="/wiki/National_Security_Council_(Antigua_and_Barbuda)" title="National Security Council (Antigua and Barbuda)">National Security Council</a> and the Financial Intelligence Unit are the two intelligence agencies that Antigua and Barbuda have at their disposal. <a href="/wiki/Camp_Blizzard" title="Camp Blizzard">Camp Blizzard</a> serves as the administrative center for the Defense Force.
</p><p>In 2018, Antigua and Barbuda signed the UN <a href="/wiki/Treaty_on_the_Prohibition_of_Nuclear_Weapons" title="Treaty on the Prohibition of Nuclear Weapons">treaty on the Prohibition of Nuclear Weapons</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-68" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-68">&#91;68&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Administrative_divisions">Administrative divisions</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Administrative divisions">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main articles: <a href="/wiki/Parishes_and_dependencies_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Parishes and dependencies of Antigua and Barbuda">Parishes and dependencies of Antigua and Barbuda</a> and <a href="/wiki/Local_government_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Local government in Antigua and Barbuda">Local government in Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<p>Antigua and Barbuda is divided into six parishes and two dependencies:<sup id="cite_ref-FactbookAttribution_55-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-FactbookAttribution-55">&#91;55&#93;</a></sup>
</p>

<div style="position: absolute;font-size:100%;left:260px;top:260px"><a href="/wiki/Saint_Peter_Parish,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Saint Peter Parish, Antigua and Barbuda">St. Peter</a></div>

<div style="position: absolute;font-size:100%;left:250px;top:300px"><a href="/wiki/Saint_Paul_Parish,_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Saint Paul Parish, Antigua and Barbuda">Saint Paul</a></div>
<div style="position: absolute;font-size:100%;left:170px;top:220px"><a href="/wiki/Antigua" title="Antigua"><i><b>ANTIGUA</b></i></a></div>
<div style="position: absolute;font-size:100%;left:90px;top:160px"><a href="/wiki/Caribbean_Sea" title="Caribbean Sea"><i><b>CARIBBEAN SEA</b></i></a></div>

</td></tr></tbody></table>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua_parishes_english.png" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/5e/Antigua_parishes_english.png/220px-Antigua_parishes_english.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="163" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/5e/Antigua_parishes_english.png/330px-Antigua_parishes_english.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/5e/Antigua_parishes_english.png/440px-Antigua_parishes_english.png 2x" data-file-width="500" data-file-height="370" /></a><figcaption>Parishes of Antigua</figcaption></figure>
<p>Although they are referred to as dependencies, both Barbuda and Redonda are actually integral parts of the state and can be thought of as administrative divisions. Simply put, "dependency" is just a title. The Redonda is a second-level administrative division that is part of the Saint John Parish's District "A." Barbuda is a local administrative division on the same level as Antigua and Barbuda, and its council is the name of its local governing body. In the year 2023, there have been discussions regarding the possibility of extending governmental authority to Antigua's parishes.
</p><p>There are currently sixty of what are known as <a href="/wiki/Major_Divisions_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" class="mw-redirect" title="Major Divisions of Antigua and Barbuda">major divisions</a> on the islands of Antigua and Barbuda. This administrative tier is known as the second level.
</p><p>Local government in the country of Antigua and Barbuda is only present on the island of Barbuda at the present time; however, there is legislation in place for a system of village councils on the island of Antigua; however, village councils have not been active since the 1940s and 1950s.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Human_rights">Human rights</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Human rights">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>As of July 2022, <a href="/wiki/LGBT_rights_in_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="LGBT rights in Antigua and Barbuda">Same-sex sexual activity</a> is legal in Antigua and Barbuda.<sup id="cite_ref-69" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-69">&#91;69&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-70" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-70">&#91;70&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Economy">Economy</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Antigua_and_Barbuda&amp;action=edit&amp;section=25" title="Edit section: Economy">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Economy_of_Antigua_and_Barbuda" title="Economy of Antigua and Barbuda">Economy of Antigua and Barbuda</a></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1021810730"><div class="mod-gallery mod-gallery-default mod-gallery-right"><div class="main"><div><ul class="gallery mw-gallery-traditional nochecker bordered-images whitebg">
		<li class="gallerybox" style="width: 235px">
			<div class="thumb" style="width: 230px; height: 143px;"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="/wiki/File:Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_(2019).svg" class="mw-file-description" title="A proportional representation of Antigua and Barbuda exports, 2019"><img alt="A proportional representation of Antigua and Barbuda exports, 2019" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/174px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png" decoding="async" width="174" height="113" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/262px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg/348px-Antigua_and_Barbuda_Product_Exports_%282019%29.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1100" data-file-height="715" /></a></span></div>

		<li class="gallerybox" style="width: 235px">
